In essence, these constitutional amendments come from the declaration of the republic to the top of the most important and fundamental changes made in our Constitution. From now on, Turkey will continue to be ruled under the foundations envisioned by the new Constitution. So far, we've been run by the parliamentary system. Legislative-walking relations were carried out according to the parliamentary system, along with parts that would not be compatible with the quality of the parliamentary system. The new system basically reveals the full discrepancy of legislation and governance, its full independence to each other. The new system commands that the executive authority be given directly by the people, that Parliament be removed from the way, that the power be based on the public's confidence, not on Parliament's trust. The new system strengthens legislation, makes lawmakers more active than the parliamentary system, strengthens lawmakers according to today's system by handing over the law enforcement activities to the Grand National Assembly of Turkey and the law proposals to lawmakers outside budget law.
